# Releasing the middle sector: exact original-row lift and two scoped failures

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Project gd-exact-profile-20260926, worker max4,
invocation81, guidance b941bd3964f8f8f6, plan8fc254f625619e19.
No formal submission was permitted or made. No new fact_id exists.

This report derives the full remainder when all middle gradients are
released at j=0,k=n-2. It gives explicit nonnegative weights on actual
rows for two natural lifts, and proves that neither lift is positive
semidefinite on the full formal space, even with the allowed objective
slack. The second mechanism tests an explicit four-sector discriminator:
its negative formal direction is not interpolable, and the most direct
single-boundary-cycle repair cannot be PSD either. These are scoped
certificate obstructions, not a counterexample to a function-class bound.

The theorem-level interface remains open: a successful lift needs a
different original-row construction with a full-space sign/Schur proof.
No uniform upper bound on W, no primal escape, and no resolution of the
original every-n/every-nonnegative-word problem is claimed.

All derivations below are joint: no new unverified lemma is imported as
an accepted premise. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies only original
interpolation necessity/sufficiency and normalization. Accepted
931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only its hierarchy/benchmark scope, in Section12.

## 1. Same physical word, actual boundary indices, and normalization

Fix any integer m>=1536, and throughout set

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon_h=1/(4m), beta_h=1-epsilon_h,
    c=lambda(1+epsilon_h), a=beta_h/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j),
    h=H_m, n=2^m-1, S=sum h, Z=D_m=1+2S.

All children keep this SAME enclosing m and all its parameters. Index
the actual steps from0 to n-1. Put

    K=n-2, R=n-1,
    A=h_0=h_R=1+a, d=a, e=h_K-1=a*c=beta_h,
    I={1,...,K}, M=sum_(i=1)^(K-1) h_i,
    B=sum_(i in I)h_i=M+1+e=S-2A,
    v=M+1=B-e, U=1+d+v,
    w=A+1/2-A^2/2=1-a^2/2,
    D=U+e+w=S+1/2-A^2/2.

Thus 2D=Z-A^2. The singleton first sector is index0, the entire middle
sector is I, the singleton last queried sector is indexR, and n is the
actual unqueried terminal. In particular h_K has dyadic level1, not0:
K+1=2^m-2 is divisible by2 but not4. Every physical leaf remains.

Normalize a legal instance to L=1, f_star=0, x_star=g_star=0. Write
x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t and use exactly the accepted orientation

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0.       (1.1)

The objective-certificate level is denoted chi, to avoid confusion with
any gradient or benchmark capacity. It would bound 2f_n/||x_0||^2.
General L follows by dividing the objective by L and translating its own
specified minimizer; the normalization stays W=2R.

Elementary bounds used below are jointly recorded here. Since
lambda<5/2 and epsilon_h<=1/6144, in fact c<5/2; for example use
sqrt(2)<10/7, lambda<17/7, and (17/7)(6145/6144)<5/2.
Also beta_h>99/100, so

    1/3<a<1/2, 1<A<3/2, 0<e<1, 7/8<w<1.             (1.2)

The upper bound on a follows from a<1/lambda=sqrt(2)-1<1/2.
For the lower bound use a>(99/100)/(5/2)=99/250>1/3.
There are n-3 ordinary middle entries, all exceeding1, so v>n-2>5.
In particular U>6 and D>18. Much weaker horizon bounds would suffice.

For later mass comparisons put X_h,j=1+sum H_j. Literal summation gives
X_h,(j+1)=2X_h,j+a*c^j, X_h,0=1, hence

    X_h,j=(1-v_h)2^j+v_h*c^j, v_h=a/(c-2).           (1.3)

Substitution verifies this symbolic formula, without evaluating a
recurrence. Since c(c-2)>lambda(lambda-2)=1>beta_h, one has 0<v_h<1.
Consequently X_h,m<c^m, S<c^m, and B<c^m.

## 2. A concrete three-sector row identity, rederived jointly

Start by imposing g_i=q for i in I, and put p=g_0, r=g_R. Define

    alpha=A/(2D), theta=(U+e)/(2D-U),
    beta=theta-alpha, gamma=1-theta,
    ell=alpha, eta=alpha/(d+v), R_flow=alpha-ell=0,
    kappa=beta+ell+eta=theta+eta,
    gbar=alpha*p+beta*q+gamma*r.                    (2.1)

Use these core weights mu on ORIGINAL rows:

| Row | mu |
|---|---:|
| Q_(0,star) | alpha |
| Q_(K,star) | beta |
| Q_(R,star) | gamma |
| Q_(K,0) | ell+eta |
| Q_(0,K) | eta |
| Q_(R,K) | beta+ell |
| Q_(R,0) | R_flow=0 |

Also put weight1 on Q_(n,R), the actual last-step row. All unlisted
weights are zero. It is convenient to retain the symbol R_flow in
some algebra so that its role as a row coefficient is explicit.

These weights are nonnegative. Indeed

    theta=(U+e)/(U+2e+2w) <1,
    theta>1/2 because U>2w,
    alpha<1/8, beta>3/8, gamma>0, eta>0, kappa>1/2. (2.2)

The value coefficient at each of0 and K is zero; at R it is1.
The last-step row moves this coefficient to n. Thus all values cancel
in chi||x_0||^2-2f_n+2sum mu Q, without assuming any formula for them.
The value balance will remain valid when the gradients are released.

The last row is the exact identity

    Q_(n,R)=f_n-f_R+(A-A^2/2)||r||^2
                            +||g_n+(A-1)r||^2/2.    (2.3)

This keeps the terminal free; its minimizing formal gradient is not
silently imposed on a legal function. It also explains the charge w.

Direct expansion on the equal-middle subspace gives

    K_0=(alpha-eta*d)||p||^2+kappa*v||q||^2+w||r||^2
       +[beta*A+ell*d+eta*(d-v)]<p,q>
       +[gamma*A+R_flow*d]<p,r>
       +[B-(beta+ell)*v]<q,r>.                    (2.4)

The polynomial after value cancellation is

    chi||x_0||^2-2<gbar,x_0>+2K_0
                                      +||g_n+(A-1)r||^2.

The two identities

    alpha*(2D-1)=A-eta*(d+v),
    theta*(2D-U)=U+e                             (2.5)

follow by substituting (2.1). Expanding K_0-D||gbar||^2 in
inc_pq=p-q and inc_qr=q-r, its common-gradient terms vanish by (2.5), and

    K_0-D||gbar||^2=J11||inc_pq||^2+J22||inc_qr||^2,
    J11=alpha-eta*d-D*alpha^2
       =alpha*[v-d*(1+v+d)]/[2(d+v)],
    J22=theta*(U-D*theta).                       (2.6)

For completeness the possible mixed entry is
gamma*(2D*alpha-A)/2=0, which follows from the first definition
in (2.1). Thus this is a whole two-increment identity, not a diagonal
test with an omitted mixed coefficient.

Both entries are positive. The numerator in J11 is
(1-d)v-d(1+d)>v/2-3/4>0. For J22, expansion gives

    U*(2D-U)-D*(U+e)=U*w-e*(e+w)>0,

because U>6,w>7/8,e<1,w<1. The denominator 2D-U is positive.

Set chi0=1/(2D)=1/(Z-A^2). The complete equal-middle identity is

    chi||x_0||^2-2f_n+2sum mu Q
      =(chi-chi0)||x_0||^2
       +2D||gbar-x_0/(2D)||^2
       +2J11||p-q||^2+2J22||q-r||^2
       +||g_n+(A-1)r||^2.                        (2.7)

The allowed chi_target=17/(16Z) is greater than chi0 because
Z>17A^2. This follows from A<3/2 and Z>2n+1, n=2^m-1.
Thus there really is nonnegative objective slack available. The failures
below take x_0=0 and therefore survive even arbitrarily large chi.

## 3. Mechanism1a: exact lift with the actual last middle anchor

Release every middle gradient, keep q=g_K, and define only algebraically

    E=sum_(i=1)^(K-1)h_i*(g_i-q).                 (3.1)

Then

    x_K=x_0-Ap-Mq-E,
    x_R=x_0-Ap-Bq-E.

Keep exactly the row weights of Section2. No interpolation row is placed
at a mean. Relative to (2.4), the displacement terms of the affected
rows are as follows:

| Row | additional term |
|---|---|
| Q_(K,star) | <q,E> |
| Q_(R,star) | <r,E> |
| Q_(K,0) | <q,E> |
| Q_(0,K) | -<p,E> |
| Q_(R,K) | 0 |
| Q_(R,0) | <r,E> |
| Q_(0,star), Q_(n,R) | 0 |

Every f_i is still independent before cancellation. In particular there
is no hidden equality f_K=f_1-M||q||^2. The coefficients of f_0,f_K,f_R
cancel by the literal balance in Section2; all other intermediate
sample values have coefficient0, not an invented value at a mean.

Consequently (2.7) holds on the ENTIRE original formal space after adding

    2<E,C>,
    C=-eta*p+kappa*q+(gamma+R_flow)*r.            (3.2)

This is the exact frozen-weight remainder. Every internal gradient is
present in E, including g_1; the last middle endpoint remains q=g_K.
There is no positive ||E||^2 term.

This frozen lift is not PSD for any finite chi. Choose a unit vector z,
x_0=0, p=q=r=z and g_n=-(A-1)z. Choose one actual internal i<K and
set g_i=q-T*z/h_i, keeping the other middle gradients q. Then E=-Tz,
C=z because -eta+kappa+gamma+R_flow=1, and the entire certificate is

                         2D-2T.

It is negative whenever T>D. This is a FORMAL direction only: it is not
claimed to satisfy the interpolation inequalities. Its purpose is the
full-space PSD test required of a dual certificate. The available
objective slack cannot repair it because x_0=0.

## 4. Mechanism1b: a legal fan of actual rows, with arbitrary probabilities

A natural attempt to create internal squares is to replace the q-node
rows by averages of ACTUAL query rows. Fix any probabilities omega_i>=0
for i in I, with sum omega_i=1. Retain alpha,beta,gamma,ell,eta,R_flow
from Section2. Use the following original weights:

    mu_(0,star)=alpha, mu_(R,star)=gamma,
    mu_(i,star)=beta*omega_i,
    mu_(i,0)=(ell+eta)*omega_i,
    mu_(0,i)=eta*omega_i,
    mu_(R,i)=(beta+ell)*omega_i          (i in I),
    mu_(R,0)=R_flow,
    mu_(n,R)=1.                                  (4.1)

All are explicit nonnegative original-row multipliers. There are no
middle-to-middle rows in this particular fan. The balance at EVERY
middle index is

    beta*omega_i+(ell+eta)*omega_i
                         -eta*omega_i-(beta+ell)*omega_i=0.

Thus all original sampled values cancel separately. No averaged value
or fictitious jet is introduced. Define algebraic moments

    P_i=sum_(l=1)^(i-1)h_l*g_l,  P_1=0,
    dvec=sum_(i in I)h_i*g_i,
    q_omega=sum_(i in I)omega_i*g_i,
    N_omega=sum_(i in I)omega_i*||g_i||^2,
    P_omega=sum_(i in I)omega_i*P_i,
    T_omega=sum_(i in I)omega_i*<g_i,P_i>,
    gbar_omega=alpha*p+beta*q_omega+gamma*r.         (4.2)

The term T_omega is chronologically ordered. It is not the square of a
mean. Expanding every row in (4.1), using x_i=x_0-Ap-P_i and
x_R=x_0-Ap-dvec, gives exactly

    chi||x_0||^2-2f_n+2sum mu Q
      =chi||x_0||^2-2<gbar_omega,x_0>+2K_omega
                                        +||g_n+(A-1)r||^2,       (4.3)

where

    K_omega=(alpha-eta*d)||p||^2+kappa*N_omega+w||r||^2
       +[beta*A+ell*d+eta*(d-1)]<p,q_omega>
       +[gamma*A+R_flow*d]<p,r>
       +<r,dvec>-eta*<p,P_omega>
       -(beta+ell)*<r,P_omega+q_omega>+kappa*T_omega.             (4.4)

One can check every coefficient directly as follows. The combined
coefficient of ||g_i||^2 is
[beta+(ell+eta)+eta+(beta+ell)]omega_i/2=kappa*omega_i.
The coefficient of <g_i,P_i> is
[beta+(ell+eta)]omega_i=kappa*omega_i.
The reverse rows Q_(0,i) contribute -eta<p,P_omega>; the Q_(R,i)
rows contribute -(beta+ell)<r,P_omega+q_omega>. All rows involving
x_R contribute exactly <r,dvec>, because
gamma+beta+ell+R_flow=1. These exhaust internal occurrences.

When omega is the point mass at K, q_omega=g_K and (4.4) reduces to
Section3 including its displacement remainder. This independently
checks the actual endpoint orientation.

## 5. Exact displacement-mean variance and chronological remainder

Now use the concrete probabilities

    omega_i=h_i/B,
    q=q_omega=dvec/B,
    nu_i=g_i-q, sum_(i in I)h_i*nu_i=0.            (5.1)

The symbol q here denotes only an algebraic displacement mean. The
legal proof expression is the original row sum (4.1), not an
interpolation row for q. Define

    H2=sum_(i in I)h_i^2,
    tau=(B^2-H2)/(2B), Delta_tau=tau-M,
    Cum_i=sum_(l=1)^i h_l,
    Z_chron=(1/B)*sum_(i in I)h_i*(B-Cum_i)*nu_i,
    J2=sum_(i in I)h_i^2*nu_i,
    V=sum_(i in I)h_i*||nu_i||^2.                 (5.2)

The exact moment identities are

    N_omega=||q||^2+V/B,
    P_omega=tau*q+Z_chron,
    T_omega=tau*||q||^2-<q,J2>/B
                         -sum_i h_i^2||nu_i||^2/(2B).           (5.3)

Proof: swap the finite sums defining P_omega to obtain its coefficient
h_i(B-Cum_i)/B. For T_omega, each unordered pair appears once with
weight h_i*h_l/B, so

    T_omega=[||sum_i h_i*g_i||^2-sum_i h_i^2||g_i||^2]/(2B).

Expand g_i=q+nu_i and use the weighted zero-sum equation. This proves
(5.3), including the term linear in J2; that term cannot be discarded.

Let K_0(p,q,r) be the polynomial (2.4), evaluated algebraically at this
mean q. Substitution in (4.4) gives the COMPLETE remainder

    K_omega=K_0(p,q,r)+R_var,

    R_var=Delta_tau*[kappa||q||^2-eta<p,q>-(beta+ell)<r,q>]
       -eta<p,Z_chron>-(beta+ell)<r,Z_chron>
       -(kappa/B)<q,J2>
       +(kappa/(2B))*sum_i h_i*(2-h_i)*||nu_i||^2.                (5.4)

Thus the complete expression (4.3) is precisely (2.7), with q now
the algebraic mean, plus 2R_var. Every formal gradient, x_0 and the
free g_n remains present. No boundary square or common-gradient
charge is duplicated. The four types of lost terms are explicit:

1. Delta_tau changes the sampled middle endpoint from physical mass M
   to the average chronological location tau.
2. Z_chron records the chronological order of every internal variation.
3. J2 records the cross term between the mean and squared step weights.
4. The remaining diagonal variance has signed weights h_i(2-h_i).

The first middle point is included with P_1=0; the last middle point is
included with its actual h_K and prefix mass M. Its contribution to
Z_chron has B-Cum_K=0. Original values disappear by separate balance,
as shown before any mean was introduced. These observations account
for the endpoints and sampled values that a collapsed argument loses.

## 6. Exact full-space obstruction to displacement-weighted row averaging

Set x_0=p=r=g_n=0. In (4.3), before any mean substitution, the complete
internal principal form is

    2*kappa*[sum_i omega_i||g_i||^2
                         +sum_(l<i)omega_i*h_l<g_i,g_l>].       (6.1)

For omega_i=h_i/B, this is

    (kappa/B)*[||sum_i h_i*g_i||^2
                              +sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||g_i||^2].      (6.2)

Both formulas follow directly from (4.4) and do not assume any common
gradient contact equations. In particular, the following obstruction
also applies to any outer coefficients of the same fan architecture
with kappa>0, whenever the displayed node balances hold.

There are two actual interior steps with h_i,h_j>2: choose the whole
root i=2^(m-1)-1 and the right-child root j=3*2^(m-2)-1. Both are in I,
and h_i=1+a*c^(m-1), h_j=1+a*c^(m-2). Bounds (1.2), c>2 and m>=1536
give the strict inequalities h_i,h_j>2 without any numeric evaluation.

For a nonzero vector z set g_i=z/h_i, g_j=-z/h_j and all other middle
gradients zero. Then sum h_l*g_l=0, and (6.2) equals

    (2*kappa/B)*(1/h_i+1/h_j-1)*||z||^2 <0.        (6.3)

This is an exact full-space negative direction at every diagnostic m.
No x_0 allowance, including 17/(16Z), changes it. A merely positive
variance based on sum h_i||nu_i||^2 is not the actual row remainder.
The chronological part contributes the negative h_i^2 weights.

For arbitrary positive omega, a two-coordinate principal minor of
(6.1), at l<i, also yields the necessary condition

    omega_l >= (h_l^2/4)*omega_i.                 (6.4)

Indeed its diagonal entries, after removing the common positive factor,
are omega_l,omega_i and its off-diagonal is h_l*omega_i/2. At the
displacement probabilities this condition becomes h_l*h_i<=4, which
fails for the two selected long steps. Non-displacement probabilities
are NOT ruled out by this argument, and (6.4) is not sufficient for
their full-space PSD. No adaptive probability family is asserted.

## 7. Mechanism2: a concrete four-sector discriminator on the same word

Let J=2^(m-1)-1 be the physical whole root, and split the actual middle
queries into I_L={1,...,J} and I_R={J+1,...,K}. Put

    B_L=sum_(i in I_L)h_i, B_R=sum_(i in I_R)h_i, B=B_L+B_R,
    V_L=sum_(i in I_L)h_i(2-h_i),
    V_R=sum_(i in I_R)h_i(2-h_i).                 (7.1)

The four queried gradients are p at0, s on I_L, t on I_R, r atR.
Its three genuine change locations are0,J,K. This discriminator is
selected by the failed middle lift; it is not another proof of an
arbitrary checkpoint-pair subclass.

Both V_L and V_R are strictly less than -c^m. To prove this, note that
their total positive linear contribution is at most2S<2c^m. I_L
contains the whole root, whose square exceeds c^(2m-2)/9 by a>1/3.
I_R contains the right-child root, whose square exceeds c^(2m-4)/9.
For m>=9, c>2 gives c^(m-4)>2^5>27, and hence each of these root
squares exceeds3c^m. Subtracting even just that square proves

    V_L<-c^m<-B, V_R<-c^m<-B.                   (7.2)

Take a nonzero z and the formal assignment

    p=r=x_0=g_n=0,
    s=z/B_L, t=-z/B_R.                          (7.3)

All three queried changes are nonzero. Its full middle displacement
is zero. On this FOUR-sector subspace the averaged certificate has
the strictly negative value

    (kappa/B)*[V_L/B_L^2+V_R/B_R^2]*||z||^2.      (7.4)

Thus the obstruction is not dependent on isolated gradient spikes or
an unbounded number of distinct queried gradients.

Crucially, (7.3) is NOT a legal primal escape. The actual first step is
x_1=x_0-Ap. The two original rows between0 and1 add to

    Q_(0,1)+Q_(1,0)
       =-d||p||^2+(d-1)<p,s>+||s||^2.           (7.5)

At (7.3) this is ||s||^2>0, contradicting interpolation. Equivalently,
for any legal first change, (7.5)<=0 is

    ||s-(1-A/2)p||^2 <= (A^2/4)||p||^2,          (7.6)

so p=0 forces s=0. The negative direction is excluded by a specific
original omitted inequality. Moreover r=0 on any legal transcript
would mean the last query and terminal are minimizers, giving zero
terminal objective. There is no asserted static function for (7.3).

## 8. The single first-boundary-cycle repair also fails, with its price paid

Test the concrete repair obtained by adding a nonnegative weight zeta
to EACH of Q_(0,1) and Q_(1,0) in the mu convention. This leaves all
values balanced and adds 2*zeta times (7.5) to the certificate. It is
the most direct actual-row repair of the violating pair just found.
All original fan weights are otherwise unchanged.

On (7.3), nonnegativity requires

    zeta >= (kappa/(2B))*(-V_L-(B_L^2/B_R^2)*V_R)
           > kappa/2 >1/4,                     (8.1)

using (7.2) and kappa>1/2.

Now test a different formal vector in the SAME four-sector space:
x_0=0, p nonzero, s=t=r=g_n=0. Formula (4.4) gives the fan value
2*(alpha-eta*d)||p||^2; (7.5) costs -d||p||^2. Nonnegativity requires

    zeta <= (alpha-eta*d)/d < alpha/d
           =A/(2D*d) <9/(4D)<1/8,               (8.2)

because A<3/2,d>1/3,D>18. This contradicts (8.1).

Therefore NO zeta>=0 makes this repaired fan PSD, already on this
four-sector subspace, for ANY objective level chi. The initial
negative charge in the cycle is essential; treating it as a free
positive ||s-p||^2 would conceal exactly this contradiction.

This is a scoped failure of one paid repair, not a proof that all
cycles, all fan probability choices, or all original multipliers fail.
Adding other rows could alter both tests. Such a new full-space
construction has not been derived in this invocation.

## 9. Complete original endpoint system for this four-sector transcript

To make the legality test explicit, here is its ENTIRE endpoint
description, including all remote rows and the free terminal. Use
sector labels a_s=0,1,2,3, with vectors

    G_0=p, G_1=s, G_2=t, G_3=r,
    L_0=A, L_1=B_L, L_2=B_R, L_3=A,
    X_0=x_0, X_(a_s+1)=X_a_s-L_a_s*G_a_s.

The actual terminal position is x_n=X_4. Define the within-query
endpoint masses

    Tcap_0=0,
    Tcap_1=B_L-h_J,
    Tcap_2=B_R-h_K,
    Tcap_3=0.                                   (9.1)

Let F_a_s denote the value at the first point of sector a_s. At any
actual queried cumulative mass tau_s in that sector the data are

    x=X_a_s-tau_s*G_a_s,
    g=G_a_s,
    f=F_a_s-tau_s*||G_a_s||^2.                  (9.2)

For a legal equal-gradient sector, the two original interpolation
directions force exactly this value formula. For a proposed transcript,
(9.2) defines all physical samples; no steps are merged or removed.

For different sectors a_s,b_s, define

    C_(a_s,b_s)=F_a_s-F_b_s
       +<G_a_s,X_b_s-X_a_s>+||G_a_s-G_b_s||^2/2,
    D_(a_s,b_s)=||G_b_s||^2-<G_a_s,G_b_s>.

The exact row from any source mass tau_s to a target mass sigma_s is

    Q=C_(a_s,b_s)+sigma_s*D_(a_s,b_s).           (9.3)

The source mass cancels. Since the target dependence is affine and
both endpoint masses0 and Tcap_b_s occur physically, ALL ordered rows
between those sectors are equivalent to the two conditions

    C_(a_s,b_s)<=0,
    C_(a_s,b_s)+Tcap_b_s*D_(a_s,b_s)<=0          (9.4)

for every ordered pair a_s!=b_s. Within-sector rows are identically
zero. The complete optimum rows are exactly

    F_a_s-<G_a_s,X_a_s>+||G_a_s||^2/2<=0,
    -F_a_s+(Tcap_a_s+1/2)||G_a_s||^2<=0.        (9.5)

Indeed the second orientation is increasing in the actual mass.

Let the free terminal gradient and value be z_n,f_n. In addition to
the actual terminal position X_4, its outgoing rows to each sector are
equivalent to both endpoint conditions sigma_s=0,Tcap_a_s in

    f_n-F_a_s+<z_n,X_a_s-X_4>+||z_n-G_a_s||^2/2
                  +sigma_s*(||G_a_s||^2-<z_n,G_a_s>) <=0.       (9.6)

The incoming rows from every sample in that sector are independent
of its source mass and reduce to

    F_a_s-f_n+<G_a_s,X_4-X_a_s>
                                      +||G_a_s-z_n||^2/2<=0.   (9.7)

Finally the two terminal-optimum rows are

    f_n-<z_n,X_4>+||z_n||^2/2<=0,
    -f_n+||z_n||^2/2<=0.                        (9.8)

Equations (9.4)--(9.8) are the whole original endpoint system, not
only neighboring inequalities. They retain all remote normals, all
values and the actual free terminal. They can be checked by (9.3)
on every physical sample. No feasibility or favorable objective is
asserted merely from writing this finite system.

For context, if vectors and values DID satisfy the entire system,
accepted1252 supplies a single static interpolant using all original
jets. With delta_j=f_j-||g_j||^2/2 and y_j=x_j-g_j, including the zero
optimum jet, it is

    H(v)=||v||^2/2+max_j[<y_j,v>-delta_j],
    F(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-H(v)].                     (9.9)

The optimum rows ensure delta_j>=0. Thus H(0)=0, the active affine
pieces at0 are exactly those with delta_j=0, and its complete minimum
set is

    argmin F=partial H(0)=conv{y_j:delta_j=0}.    (9.10)

This last equality follows directly from the directional derivative of
a finite maximum at0, together with the zero derivative of ||v||^2/2;
x is a minimizer of F exactly when0 maximizes <x,v>-H(v), equivalently
x is a subgradient of H at0. The set includes0 from the optimum jet.
Its true initial squared radius would be

    dist(x_0,conv{y_j:delta_j=0})^2.             (9.11)

These formulas are an exact conditional completion/radius interface,
NOT an initialized adversary or a radius value for (7.3). That formal
direction already fails (9.4) at the first boundary, so applying the
interpolant construction to it is unjustified.

## 10. What a successful next lift must actually supply

The frozen and mean-weighted identities locate the missing terms rather
than suppressing them. Neither a diagonal-variance claim nor a choice
of the available chi slack pays them.

A new proof must give nonnegative original weights with exact value
balance and a full-space bound. It could change the middle probability
law, add actual middle-to-middle rows, or recursively distribute flow,
but none of those mechanisms is completed here. For a fan with a
different probability law, even its internal two-coordinate blocks
must satisfy (6.4), and the chronological vectors P_omega,T_omega and
the independent displacement dvec still remain in (4.4). A successful
recursion cannot keep only the displacement mean while forgetting
these moments. Its boundary state and sign closure must be explicit.

The two formal tests in Section8 show why the first missing adjacent
cycle cannot simply be assigned whatever weight repairs (7.4): its
initial charge must also be paid. They do not exclude a larger network
that supplies that price through other rows. No unspecified PSD block
is offered as a theorem, and no unknown block has been assigned a
positive sign.

No new substantive mechanism is begun after these two permitted ones.

## 11. Literature grounding and retrieval limits

One Matlas query asked for a smooth-convex, nonconstant-stepsize
interpolation certificate with an arbitrary-gradient variance lift.
Its three results did not provide that interface: their hypotheses
involved a PL condition, stochastic subgradients, or short-step
self-contractedness. They are not premises.

The fallback search identified Jason Altschuler and Pablo Parrilo,
"Acceleration by Stepsize Hedging II: Silver Stepsize Schedule for
Smooth Convex Optimization", arXiv:2309.16530. The official PDF and
HTML were downloaded under downloads/release_middle_sector/. Theorem
3.4, its original gluing/rank-one/sparse corrections, Lemmas3.6--3.7,
and the proof of the gluing theorem were read in extracted official
HTML text. No theorem from that paper is used as a correctness premise.
Its exact schedule, coefficients, row orientation and rate invariant
are not automatically those of this inflated same-m hierarchy.

The relevant methodological point is limited: that proof keeps internal
rows and proves the signs of their combined corrections. It does not
justify replacing actual gradients by a sampled mean. Our obstruction
concerns the explicitly written fans (3.2),(4.1), not that gluing
theorem or all recursive certificates. No extra literature is needed
to check the purely algebraic negative directions in those fixed fans.

Retrieval issues were recorded honestly. The first prior-report path
was mistyped; the listed correct ancestor_bridge_report.md was then
read. The local known-paper filename search returned no match.
pdftotext was unavailable, as were installed Python PDF extractors.
Official arXiv HTML was therefore obtained and converted to text with
the standard-library HTMLParser. No installation or mathematical
program was run.

## 12. Exact target status and handoff

Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 proves, for this SAME hierarchy and every
m>=1536, B_n/Z<47/50. This is an UPPER benchmark comparison. It would
turn a full-class W_n(H_m)<=17/(16Z) into a negative resolution of the
original asymptotic question, but that hypothesis remains UNPROVED.

This invocation gives complete joint draft identities (3.2),(4.4),(5.4),
the exact scoped PSD failures (6.3),(7.4),(8.1)--(8.2), and the complete
four-sector original endpoint system (9.4)--(9.8). All are UNVERIFIED.
They are findings for future proof search, not accepted building blocks.

Original target: OPEN. Full function-class threshold: OPEN.
Primal counterexample to that threshold: NOT PRODUCED.
Formal submissions, math/numeric programs, recurrence/trajectory replays,
optimizers, installations, paid consults and subagents: ZERO.
Mechanisms used: TWO. Automatic renewal: NO.

# An intrinsic affine child with a nonzero endpoint residual

Status: **UNVERIFIED joint proof candidate**, worker max, completed-round
target 27. Nothing in this document is a new accepted fact. This round uses
no solver, model evaluation, word scan, arithmetic replay, installation,
subagent, or verifier call. All new arguments are given together below;
earlier unverified manuscripts are construction leads, not premises.

Accepted fact `1252b8823bb5a290` supplies the original interpolation
inequalities and their constructive sufficiency. Accepted fact
`b98bb807d514e80d` supplies the exact benchmark notation. The original
arbitrary-schedule exact-profile problem remains unresolved.

## 1. The change, the finite result, and its scope

The fixed31 affine-top separator in shared finding `818dec6f7e4d93fa`
uses queried anchors with residual zero, equal intrinsic heights at those
anchors, and specific internal return costs and correlations. Here the
RIGHT COPY INSIDE THE FIRST SEVEN-STEP CHILD is physically translated and
contracted. At its endpoint the residual is a nonzero vector t, and its
intrinsic height is -<g,t>, not the height at the other atomic endpoint.
Thus the equal-zero-residual-anchor premise is absent before a later
parent is even considered. This is neither a coordinate change nor a
change confined to the final unqueried sample.

The resulting seven-step transcript is explicit, legal for every
0<sigma<=1, and realized by one global convex 1-smooth function on R^3.
For sigma<1 the minimizer set of the particular interpolant constructed
here is exactly the segment [0,t]. Zero is its TRUE closest minimizer to
the initial point. Every original ordered interpolation pair is checked.

For the concrete choice sigma=9999/10000, the child's capacity-normalized
endpoint state satisfies

    kappa_7 = sigma^2 kappa_3^2 > 1+1/1500.                 (1)

Nevertheless, if this fixed seven-step child is joined to another scaled
copy by the next specified origin-aligned bridge, with its supplied
values retained, EVERY transverse coupling fails. In fact, throughout
9999/10000<=sigma<=1 a positive combination of just TWO original queried
rows has the exact lower bound

    Q_(6,13) + c_sigma Q_(3,13)
        > beta^2 Delta_5 / 7500 > 0,                      (2)

where c_sigma=(1-sigma)r_0/(r_1 rho), beta is the necessarily matched
next scale, and Delta_5>0 is defined below. For sigma<1 both coefficients
are positive; at sigma=1 the second coefficient is zero.

The later obstruction allows arbitrary ambient dimension and every
real positive-semidefinite cross-Gram completion compatible with the
bridge. It fixes the displayed child data and the copied function values,
and sets the RELATIVE TRANSLATION OF THE NEXT TOP JOIN to zero. It does
not exclude reselecting those values, a new relative top translation,
changing the first coupling, or a larger first contraction. These
limitations differ from the broader fixed31 affine-top closeout.

The word length is really 7 and the attempted next length is really 15.
The capacity 1+sum h is not the original benchmark denominator B_n.
Equation (1) is a boundary-state surplus, not a matching lower bound for
the original minimax problem. Section 9 gives the exact actual payoff.

## 2. The original pair identity and the two-vector atom

All inner products and norms are Euclidean. Smoothness is normalized to
1. The optimum sample is (x_star,g_star,f_star)=(0,0,0). For sampled
jets define

    y_i=x_i-g_i,
    Delta_i=f_i-||g_i||^2/2,
    aheight_i=f_i-<g_i,x_i>+||g_i||^2/2.

The original convention from fact `1252b8823bb5a290` is

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

Direct expansion, without any value restriction, gives

    Q_ij=aheight_i-Delta_j+<g_i,y_j>,
    Q_(i,star)=aheight_i, Q_(star,i)=-Delta_i.             (3)

Put

    s=sqrt(2), lambda=1+s, theta=s-1=1/lambda,
    a=(311s-16)/300, b=(590+8s)/300, rho=599/600,
    z=b-1, K=a+rho, m=a+b, eta=z/K,
    A=1+2a+b=(143+105s)/50,
    r_0=theta A=(67+38s)/50, r_1=A.

The letters A and r_j denote physical capacities and bridge offsets, not
the exact all-split benchmark. In R^2 choose U,V with

    <U,V>=0, ||U||=1, ||V||^2=eta,
    w=rho V, p=mU+K V.

The atom has the exact three-step word (a,b,a), and its four jets have

    g=(U,U,V,w),
    x=(p,bU+K V,K V,w),
    f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2, 0<=i<=3.                 (4)

These positions obey all three GD updates. Its residuals and positive
energies are

    y_0=(m-1)U+K V, E_0=m-1,
    y_1=zU+K V,     E_1=z,
    y_2=(K-1)V,     E_2=(K-1)eta,
    y_3=0,         E_3=0,

where E_i=<g_i,y_i>=Delta_i and aheight_i=0. Its complete normal table
against the vertices 0,U,V,w is

| receiver i | vertex 0 | vertex U | vertex V | vertex w |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 0 | -(m-1) | 0 | -a | -a-(1-rho)z |
| 1 | -z | 0 | 0 | -(1-rho)z |
| 2 | -(K-1)eta | -(K-1)eta | 0 | -(1-rho)(K-1)eta |
| 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

An entry in row i and vertex v is <v-g_i,y_i>. Both copies of the
vertex U, at actual indices 0 and 1, are covered separately by the same
entry. All numbers in the table are nonpositive: a>1,b>2,K>1 and
0<rho<1. Thus (3) checks every atomic ordered pair and every optimum
pair, not just adjacent pairs.

Write

    R^2=||p||^2=m^2+Kz,
    N=||w||^2=rho^2 z/K,
    D=<p,w>=rho z,
    Dlen=m+K=A-(1-rho).

The atom can also be realized by the explicit projection function

    F_atom(x)=max_(v in conv(0,U,V,w))(<x,v>-||v||^2/2).

The maximizer is the Euclidean projection, and the table gives its
optimality inequalities at every sample. Completing the square and
adding the two projection inequalities gives a unique maximizer and a
1-Lipschitz gradient. The function is finite and convex. Its minimizers
form the polar cone of the hull. Since p=mU+K V is a positive combination
of gradients, <p,z_star><=0 for every such minimizer; hence the true
initial distance is R, attained at zero.

The atom's doubled terminal gap is N. Define its boundary state by

    kappa_3=A^2 N/R^2.                                   (5)

Here is an exact algebraic certificate, derived inside this joint proof,
for the strict bound needed below. The defining quantities satisfy

    m=(574+319s)/300, K=(567+622s)/600,
    z=(145+4s)/150,
    R^2=(620189+458670s)/90000,
    N=358801(145+4s)/[90000(567+622s)].

Multiplying these expressions and using s^2=2 gives

    kappa_3-1=(-8324118905+8781249146s)
                     /(2305581607500+1614558620000s).

The denominator is positive. Two thousand times the numerator minus
the denominator is

    15947939672000s-18953819417500
      >15947939672000(7/5)-18953819417500
      =3373296123300>0.

Here s>7/5 follows by squaring positive numbers. Hence

    kappa_3>1+1/2000.                                    (6)

These displayed finite identities are algebra in the candidate proof,
not a numerical experiment or independent verifier acceptance.

## 3. The actual intrinsic affine seven-step construction

Fix ANY sigma with 0<sigma<=1 and put

    delta=1-sigma, chi=delta/sigma,
    alpha=sigma r_0 sqrt(N)/R.

Here delta is a scalar contraction deficit, distinct from the indexed
function-value quantities Delta_i. Set e=w/sqrt(N), b_axis=p/R. Work in
the three-dimensional Euclidean direct sum

    R direct-sum e^perp direct-sum b_axis^perp.

Define two actual linear isometries from R^2 by

    L u=(<u,e>,u-<u,e>e,0),
    T v=(-<v,b_axis>,0,v-<v,b_axis>b_axis).

They satisfy the exact cross identity

    <L u,alpha T v>=-sigma r_0 <u,w><p,v>/R^2.           (7)

Let the physical translation be

    t=-delta r_0 Lw.

For 0<=i<=3, the seven-step parent jets, denoted by capitals in this
section, are

    X_i=Lx_i,       G_i=Lg_i,       F_i=f_i,
    X_(4+i)=t+alpha T x_i,
    G_(4+i)=alpha T g_i,            F_(4+i)=alpha^2 f_i.  (8)

The original step list is

    h_7=(a,b,a,1+r_0,a,b,a).                             (9)

There is one physical trajectory. Indeed the bridge from X_3=Lw lands
at -r_0 Lw, and

    X_4=t+alpha T p=-delta r_0 Lw-sigma r_0 Lw=-r_0 Lw.

Each within-child update follows from (4) and linearity. The right
values in (8) are deliberately the scaled atomic values. They are NOT
the projection-saturated values associated with the translated points.
This distinction is required both for feasibility and for the payoff.

The residuals and shifted values obey

    Y_i=Ly_i, Delta_i=E_i, aheight_i=0,             i<4,
    Y_(4+i)=t+alpha T y_i,
    Delta_(4+i)=alpha^2 E_i,
    aheight_(4+i)=-<G_(4+i),t>.                          (10)

In particular

    X_7=G_7+t, Y_7=t, F_7=||G_7||^2/2.

For delta>0, Y_7 is nonzero; sample 3 still has Y_3=0. Moreover

    <G_7,t>=delta sigma r_0^2 N D/R^2>0,
    aheight_7=-delta sigma r_0^2 N D/R^2<0.              (11)

These are intrinsic changes in queried data when this object is used as
a child of a larger word. In particular the two queried atomic endpoints
no longer have equal intrinsic heights.

## 4. Every original seven-step interpolation pair

The within-left pairs are the atomic table. Translation cancels from a
within-right pair, so every such Q equals alpha^2 times its atomic Q.
For all atomic indices i,j, (3) and (10) give the two cross orientations

    Q_(i,4+j)=-alpha^2 E_j+<Lg_i,t+alpha T y_j>,
    Q_(4+i,j)=-<alpha T g_i,t>-E_j+<alpha T g_i,Ly_j>.   (12)

Every atom gradient and residual has nonnegative U,V coordinates. Thus
<g_i,w>, <w,y_j>, <p,g_i>, and <p,y_j> are nonnegative. Equation (7)
then makes both mixed terms in (12) nonpositive. Also

    <Lg_i,t>=-delta r_0 <g_i,w><=0,
    <alpha T g_i,t>
        =delta sigma r_0^2 N <p,g_i>/R^2>=0.

Consequently both expressions in (12) are nonpositive for every i,j,
including all endpoint directions.

The optimum rows follow from (3): every Delta_i is nonnegative, every
left aheight is zero, and every right aheight is nonpositive. This checks
all 72 distinct ordered pairs on {star,0,...,7}: 24 within-child pairs,
32 cross pairs, and 16 optimum incidences. No pair or original gradient
has been removed. The argument works for the whole interval 0<sigma<=1.

## 5. One global function and its exact minimizer set

Define on the same R^3

    H(v)=||v||^2/2+max(0,max_(0<=i<=7)(<Y_i,v>-Delta_i)),
    F(X)=sup_v(<X,v>-H(v)).                              (13)

The function H is finite, continuous, 1-strongly convex and has
quadratic growth. The supremum has a unique maximizer. The explicit
interpolation construction in accepted fact `1252b8823bb5a290`, applied
to the full original pair check above, proves that F is finite,
everywhere differentiable, convex and 1-smooth, and

    F(X_i)=F_i, grad F(X_i)=G_i, F(0)=0=min F.

Thus all eight samples belong to ONE function fixed before GD starts.
The construction does not paste separate functions along the run.

For clarity its full minimizer set can be computed directly. All six
nonendpoint Delta_i are strictly positive; the two endpoint quantities
Delta_3 and Delta_7 are zero and their slopes in (13) are 0 and t.
Thus, along any fixed v and for all sufficiently small epsilon>0,

    H(epsilon v)=epsilon^2||v||^2/2
                          +epsilon max(0,<t,v>).        (14)

For any X in [0,t], <X,v><=max(0,<t,v>) for every v.
Equation (13) then gives F(X)<=0; the choice v=0 gives F(X)>=0.
Conversely, if X lies outside that segment, take its closest point z on
the segment and v=X-z. Projection onto a segment gives
<v,z'-z><=0 for every z' in the segment, whereas <v,X-z>=||v||^2>0.
Hence <X,v>>max(0,<t,v>). Equation (14), with epsilon sufficiently small,
gives <X,epsilon v>-H(epsilon v)>0, so F(X)>0. We have proved

    argmin F=[0,t].                                     (15)

This remains valid at sigma=1, when the segment is the singleton {0}.
The exact TRUE initial radius is R because

    <Lp,t>=-delta r_0 D<=0,
    ||Lp-q t||^2=R^2+q^2||t||^2-2q<Lp,t>>=R^2
                                                    for 0<=q<=1.

Zero attains this minimum. No normalization has substituted distance to
a convenient, more distant minimizer.

## 6. Exact state law, including the cost of translation

The physical capacity of (9) is

    A_1=1+sum h_7=2A+r_0=lambda A.

Let w_1=G_7 and N_1=||w_1||^2=alpha^2 N. The actual doubled terminal
gap is J_1=2F(X_7)=N_1. Therefore

    V_7=2(F(X_7)-min F)/dist(X_0,argmin F)^2
       =alpha^2 N/R^2,
    kappa_7=A_1^2 V_7=sigma^2 kappa_3^2.                (16)

For sigma>=9999/10000, (6) gives

    sigma kappa_3 > (9999/10000)(2001/2000)
                   =1+7999/20000000 > 1+1/3000,

and hence (1) follows by squaring. This prices the contracted right
copy: its loss is precisely the factor sigma^2.

The endpoint shape has the exact two parameters

    tau_1=<w_1,t>/N_1=chi D/N,
    nu_1=||t||^2/N_1=chi^2 R^2/N.                       (17)

At the next bridge 1+r_1, the outgoing point is

    d=t-r_1 w_1,
    ||d||^2=N_1(r_1^2-2r_1 tau_1+nu_1).                (18)

The vector d is nonzero for sigma>0. Indeed the first isometry T sends
w to a vector with a nonzero b_axis-perpendicular component, because p
has a nonzero U component and w has none. The vector t has zero in that
last direct-sum component. Thus r_1 w_1 cannot equal t.

If an origin-aligned scaled-copy next join were legal with its copied
values, its forced scale would be beta=||d||/R, its capacity would be
A_2=lambda A_1, and its formal endpoint law would be

    kappa_15,formal
       =(1-2tau_1/r_1+nu_1/r_1^2) kappa_7^2.           (19)

This is a conditional arithmetic state law, not a claim of legality.
It even has a surplus in the interval used here. The elementary bounds
N>39/100,D<11/10 and chi<=1/9999 imply
tau_1<3/9999<1/3000. Since r_1>5, the factor in (19) exceeds
1-1/7500. Also kappa_7^2>1+1/750, so
(1-1/7500)(1+1/750)>1. Thus the formal next state exceeds 1.
The following original rows explain why this positive number does not
belong to an actual matched fifteen-step trajectory.

## 7. The exact two-row obstruction, with every transverse map free

In this section abbreviate the seven-step data by (X_i,G_i,F_i) as in
(8), and let P=X_0=Lp. Preserve all these data in each child. Consider
arbitrary linear isometries E,T_next from their R^3 into any common real
Euclidean space, and the fifteen-step candidate

    left jets:  (E X_i,E G_i,F_i),              0<=i<=7,
    right jets: (beta T_next X_i,beta T_next G_i,beta^2 F_i),
                                                     0<=i<=7,
    word: h_15=(h_7,1+r_1,h_7).

There is no relative translation in THIS NEXT JOIN. Its physical bridge
requires

    beta T_next P=E d, beta^2=||d||^2/R^2.              (20)

All orientations satisfying (20) are allowed. In particular we impose
no sign, rank, symmetry or sparsity condition on their remaining cross
inner products.

The retained child has G_3=Lw, G_6=alpha T V and G_7=rho G_6. Thus

    t=-delta r_0 G_3,
    d=-delta r_0 G_3-r_1 rho G_6,
    G_6+c_sigma G_3=-d/(r_1 rho),
    c_sigma=delta r_0/(r_1 rho)>=0.                     (21)

For its local sample 5 put

    Delta_5=alpha^2 z>0,
    Y_5=t+alpha T(zU+K V),
    H_sigma=delta+sigma z Dlen/R^2.

Equations (7) and (10) give, by direct multiplication,

    <P,Y_5>=-r_0 D H_sigma,
    aheight_3=0,
    aheight_6=-<G_6,t>=-chi Delta_5.                    (22)

Apply the universally valid identity (3) to original parent rows (6,13)
and (3,13). Their common receiver 13 is the right local sample 5. Use
(20)-(22) to get the exact cancellation

    S_sigma:=Q_(6,13)+c_sigma Q_(3,13)
      =-chi Delta_5-(1+c_sigma)beta^2 Delta_5
                         -beta^2<P,Y_5>/(r_1 rho).

Consequently

    S_sigma/(beta^2 Delta_5)
      =r_0 D H_sigma/(r_1 rho alpha^2 z)
                         -1-c_sigma-chi/beta^2.          (23)

There are no remaining free transverse inner products. Both source
indices 3,6 and the receiver 13 are queried in the actual length-15
word. No optimum row, incoming final row, or deleted comparison was
used. Arbitrarily changing the unqueried final gradient cannot repair
these two queried rows.

Here are explicit scalar formulas valid for EVERY 0<sigma<=1, not only
the range in (2). Define

    C_aff=K Dlen/(r_0 r_1 rho^2).

Using D=rho z and alpha^2=sigma^2 r_0^2 N/R^2 in (23), the first term is

    r_0 D H_sigma/(r_1 rho alpha^2 z)
      =C_aff/sigma+delta R^2/(r_0 r_1 sigma^2 N).         (24)

Expansion of (18) also gives

    beta^2=(r_0^2 N/R^2)
        [delta^2+sigma^2 r_1^2 N/R^2
                            -2delta sigma r_1 D/R^2].   (25)

Therefore any legal next join in this precisely stated class must obey

    C_aff/sigma+delta R^2/(r_0 r_1 sigma^2 N)
       <=1+delta r_0/(r_1 rho)+delta/(sigma beta^2).      (26)

This is a necessary test, not a sufficient system or a claim that a
larger contraction produces a legal parent.

## 8. A rational positive margin on the selected affine family

We now prove (2) for 9999/10000<=sigma<=1. Squaring rational endpoints
gives 14142/10000<s<14143/10000. Substitution in the definitions yields

    K>2.411, Dlen>5.828, A<5.8301, theta<.4143,
    rho^2<.9967.

All decimals here are exact terminating rationals. Since r_0 r_1=theta A^2,

    K Dlen>2.411*5.828=14.051308,
    r_0 r_1 rho^2<.4143*(5.8301)^2*.9967<14.036,
    14.036*(1+1/1000)=14.050036<14.051308.

It follows that

    C_aff>1+1/1000.                                    (27)

The coarse estimates needed for the scale are

    r_0>12/5, 29/5<r_1<6, N>39/100,
    R^2<15, D<11/10, r_0/(r_1 rho)<1/2.                 (28)

For example K<243/100,z>1 give eta>2/5 and hence
N>(99/100)^2(2/5)>39/100. The bounds m<344/100,
K<243/100,z<101/100 give R^2<15. The exact expression
r_0=(67+38s)/50 and s>7/5 give r_0>12/5. The last bound in (28) is
theta/rho<1/2, since 2theta<.8286<rho. The remaining estimates follow
by direct substitution in A and D.

From (25), discarding its nonnegative delta^2 term, we obtain

    beta^2 >= (r_0^2 N/R^4)
                       [sigma^2 r_1^2 N-2delta sigma r_1 D].

Here sigma^2>999/1000 and delta<=1/10000. Thus the bracket is strictly
larger than

    (999/1000)(841/25)(39/100)-2(1/10000)6(11/10)>13.

Consequently

    beta^2 > ((144/25)(39/100)/225)*13
            =73008/562500 >1/8,                        (29)

where the last comparison is 584064>562500.

Combining (23)-(24) with (27)-(29) gives

    S_sigma/(beta^2 Delta_5)
      >1/1000-8/9999-1/20000 >1/7500.                  (30)

For the last comparison, 1/1000-1/20000-1/7500=49/60000 and
49*9999=489951>480000=8*60000. This proves the claimed strictly positive
combination of two original interpolation inequalities.

Thus the whole small-contraction interval is excluded at its next
origin-aligned matched join, including the explicit nonzero-residual
child sigma=9999/10000. A positive scalar state alone has again failed
to establish a global interpolating function, but this obstruction acts
on different queried geometry from the former zero-residual child.

## 9. Objective normalization, original indices, and remaining freedom

For the actual legal seven-step child the exact original normalized
objective is

    V_7=sigma^2 r_0^2 N^2/R^4
       =sigma^2 kappa_3^2/(lambda^2 A^2)>0,
    W_7(h_7)>=V_7,
    V_7/U_7^ZJ=B_7 sigma^2 kappa_3^2/(lambda^2 A^2).     (31)

Here W is the original W=2R normalized objective quantity (the R in
that project convention is not the radius R used in our displayed
coordinates). Specifically the numerator in (31) is twice the function
gap, and its denominator is the TRUE squared initial distance computed
in section 5. B_7=2S_bullet(7)+1 is the original all-split denominator
from accepted fact `b98bb807d514e80d`. It is not replaced by A_1 or A_1^2.
Multiplication of the function by any desired smoothness constant
L_smooth>0 gives the same normalized payoff for the prescribed steps
h_i/L_smooth. More explicitly, the function X->F(RX)/R^2, started at
X_0/R, has initial distance one and the same step list and payoff.

All displayed physical steps are strictly positive. No zero step was
inserted to change a horizon, and the final gradient at index 7 is a
genuine gradient of (13). If a zero step is explicitly inserted, retain
its duplicate actual jet and every original pair; those extra identical
jet comparisons are zero and all other comparisons are already checked.
This elementary observation is not a profile comparison at the changed
horizon. The attempted next word retains all original indices 0,...,15;
the two obstructing pairs are inside its queried prefix 0,...,14.

The construction uses one global optimum set and one global function.
It changes the internal affine placement, endpoint residual, and actual
intrinsic values of a queried child. The return costs have not been
declared free, and their effect is visible in the exact terms chi/beta^2
and c_sigma in (23).

The result does not decide a more substantially contracted first join:
0<sigma<9999/10000 is subject to (26), but passing (26) is not proof of
all other rows. It does not decide a different first transverse map,
free within-child value selection, or another relative translation at
the next top join. In particular, the fully affine actual15 problem for
these CHANGED children is outside the present obstruction. It should
not be confused with the completed fixed31 fully affine obstruction.

This finite special word supplies no arbitrary-h lower-bound mechanism,
no all-depth legal growth law, no upper bound on unrestricted W_15, and
no decision of the original exact-profile target. The relevant new
information is the explicit legal affine boundary state, its paid
positive surplus, and the dimension-independent two-row obstruction
to the stated small-contraction continuation.

## 10. Audit, literature, and handoff

The recalled leads were `818dec6f7e4d93fa` (fixed31 fully affine-top
closeout) and `d91691f8880332e1` (a different nonzero-terminal-residual
family). Neither is a mathematical predecessor. The present atom,
affine child, interpolant, true distance, and separator are rederived
in this one manuscript. A monotone auxiliary quadratic repair was
not pursued because shared findings `ab39085b21b02377` and
`5449b0b644f2a9f3` already price and exclude that different route.

The direct screen initially proposed adding endpoint-7 rows to
Q_(6,13). Its two residual terms have the same sign, so that proposed
cancellation was rejected. The proof uses instead the exact positive
gradient combination (21), which cancels the mixed term with Q_(3,13).
No claim relies on the discarded identity.

Scope checks retain sigma=1 as a limiting case, keep the genuinely
nonzero residual for sigma<1, and retain both actual minimizers 0,t.
At a smaller or differently translated next bridge, identities (24)-(25)
must be changed; (30) is not asserted there. The source function stays
legal at every 0<sigma<=1, so the obstruction is to the next specified
join, not to the existence of affine nonempty children.

Literature search was conditioned on intrinsic affine interpolation.
Matlas did not supply a new applicable result. The exact local copy of
Taylor--Hendrickx--Glineur, arXiv:1502.05666, Theorem 4 proof, Corollary 1,
and Remark 3 was read to audit the finite interpolation and cycle
conventions. No external theorem is imported: the sole realization
predecessor is accepted fact `1252b8823bb5a290`. The source is
https://arxiv.org/abs/1502.05666.

This geometry lane is distinct from max3's shared plan
`1c55f2ca09562670`, which varies a conditional three-sign distribution
and its real character correlations. Equation (21) explains the shared
boundary: changing a probabilistic description without changing that
actual positive gradient relation and the receiver jet would retain
the two-row obstruction. No exclusion of max3's distinct construction
is inferred.

All new mathematical claims remain UNVERIFIED. No fact_submit was
authorized by TASK.md or guidance `98d603742ebc21e3`, and none was called.
This completes the one permitted new round at completed count 27,
attempted count 28 including the preserved operator interruption. It
does not create an additional round or any numerical/verifier allowance.

# Quantitative queried cuts of full objective certificates

Status: complete self-contained candidate calculations, NOT verifier-accepted.
Current TASK/master_guidance closes routine verification and numerical search;
neither a solver nor fact_submit is used. Every new calculation below belongs
to this single proof package, not to a chain of unverified premises. The only
accepted mathematical dependency is fact `1252b8823bb5a290`, for the exact
normalized problem, interpolation necessity, and attained full certificates.

## 1. The unchanged target and the full certificate hypothesis

For a finite nonnegative schedule h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), W_n(h) is the
supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over every finite
dimension, convex differentiable f with L-Lipschitz gradient and a minimizer
x_star, and x_0 != x_star, using x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L) grad f(x_i).
The exact benchmark is U_n^ZJ=1/B_n, B_n=2S_bullet(n)+1, with the original
phi/psi recursions in accepted fact `b98bb807d514e80d`. The affirmative
target remains: for every epsilon>0, all sufficiently large n and EVERY
nonnegative length-n schedule have W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n. A negative
answer requires a fixed positive relative improvement along unbounded
horizons by actual full-objective upper bounds. Neither outcome is claimed.

Work at smoothness one and let x_0,g_0,...,g_n be independent formal vectors.
Set x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. All finite f_i
are independent formal scalars. Put I={star,0,...,n}, lambda_ii=0, and

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

A supplied full certificate consists of a finite real q_cert and ACTUAL
lambda_ij>=0 for which

 M=q_cert||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij Q_ij                 (1)

has every value coefficient zero and is PSD on the full original formal
space. In particular the finite-node flows obey

 out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n).                                         (2)

No optimum-source edge is forbidden. No support, symmetrizability,
positive-definiteness, h_i<2, or Huber equality assumption is made.
Zero-step gradients remain independent.

Necessity of Q_ij<=0 on legal instances (accepted1252b8823bb5a290) gives
W_n(h)<=q_cert for any such certificate. Conversely, that SAME accepted
fact supplies a full certificate at q_cert=W_n(h) for every nonnegative
h, including empty/all-zero schedules, by positive-step attainment and
explicit zero-step lifting. Thus any universal necessary inequality proved
below can be applied to at least one attained certificate at the actual W.
An inequality involving the multipliers of a particular supplied certificate
is not by itself a multiplier-free lower bound on W.

Every feasible q_cert is positive. Setting all gradients to zero first
gives q_cert>=0. Summing (2) yields
sum_i lambda_(i,star)=2+sum_i lambda_(star,i)>=2. If q_cert=0, set all
finite gradients to the same scalar1. The coefficient of scalar x_0 in
M is then -sum_i lambda_(i,star)<0, so M is unbounded below. This excludes0.

## 2. A nontrivial queried-cut capacity inequality

Fix a queried index j<n, write b_cut=h_j, and let T_cut={j+1,...,n}.
Its complement in I includes the optimum and indices0,...,j. Define

 s_tail=sum_(j<t<n)h_t,
 ell_tail(k)=sum_(j<t<k)h_t for k in T_cut,
 C_in=sum_(i outside T_cut,k in T_cut)lambda_ik,
 C_out=sum_(i in T_cut,k outside T_cut)lambda_ik,
 F_cut=sum_(k in T_cut)lambda_(j,k),
 B_cut=sum_(k in T_cut)lambda_(k,j),
 I_cut=sum_(i!=j)lambda_(i,j),
 J_forward=sum_(k in T_cut)ell_tail(k) lambda_(j,k),
 J_in=sum_(i outside T_cut,k in T_cut)ell_tail(k) lambda_ik,
 mu_query=lambda_(j,star),
 mu_tail=sum_(i in T_cut)lambda_(i,star).

These are actual array sums, including every remote edge and optimum edge.
Summing (2) over T_cut gives C_out-C_in=2. Set

 A_cut=I_cut-b_cut F_cut,
 D_cut=1+2s_tail+C_in+J_in,
 K_cut=b_cut C_out-F_cut-B_cut-J_forward.                       (3)

The following matrix is necessarily PSD:

              [ q_cert       -mu_query/2       -mu_tail/2 ]
 T_query   =  [ -mu_query/2    A_cut             K_cut/2   ].    (4)
              [ -mu_tail/2    K_cut/2            D_cut     ]

Proof. Evaluate (1) on the formal subspace

 x_0=z,  g_i=0 (i<j),  g_j=u,  g_i=v (i>j).

All value coefficients have already canceled, so they may be set to0.
Only finite-source edges to star contribute z; their total is
-<z,mu_query*u+mu_tail*v>. The coefficient of ||u||^2 in the norm
terms is (out_j+in_j)/2=I_cut, since j<n. The only additional diagonal
terms are -h_j lambda_(j,k)||u||^2 for k>j. This gives A_cut.

For the ||v||^2 coefficient, set u=z=0. A tail point k then has
x_k=-ell_tail(k)v, and a non-tail finite point has position0. Within-tail
position terms sum to

 sum_(k in T_cut)ell_tail(k)(out_k^internal-in_k^internal).

Use (2) to replace the internal divergence by
2*1_(k=n)-out_k^cross+in_k^cross. The outgoing cross position terms
add back sum_k ell_tail(k)out_k^cross. This leaves
2s_tail+J_in. The cross norm terms are
(C_out+C_in)/2=1+C_in; internal norm terms are zero. Hence D_cut.

For the coefficient of <u,v>, norm terms give -(F_cut+B_cut).
An edge j->k with k in T_cut contributes -ell_tail(k)<u,v>, totaling
-J_forward. An edge sourced in T_cut and targeting its complement
has the position shift +b_cut*u, totaling b_cut C_out<u,v>.
All other position terms have either zero source gradient or cancel
the common tail shift. These are exactly K_cut. Thus the restriction is

 q_cert||z||^2-<z,mu_query*u+mu_tail*v>
                   +A_cut||u||^2+K_cut<u,v>+D_cut||v||^2,

whose scalar coefficient matrix is (4). Since the original polynomial
is PSD on every formal vector, its restriction is PSD. This restriction
is a necessary test, not an assertion that its formal vectors are legal
trajectories, and it deletes no edge from the original certificate.

Complete the independent z square, using q_cert>0. Put

 a_cut=A_cut-mu_query^2/(4q_cert),
 d_cut=D_cut-mu_tail^2/(4q_cert),
 k_cut=K_cut-mu_query*mu_tail/(2q_cert).

Then (4) is equivalent to

 a_cut>=0, d_cut>=0, k_cut^2<=4a_cut*d_cut.                     (5)

In particular, when d_cut>0 the quantitative incidence cost is

 I_cut >= b_cut F_cut + mu_query^2/(4q_cert)
              + [b_cut C_out-F_cut-B_cut-J_forward
                       -mu_query*mu_tail/(2q_cert)]^2/(4d_cut). (6)

When d_cut=0 the square numerator in (6) must be zero and the remaining
condition is I_cut>=b_cut F_cut+mu_query^2/(4q_cert). There is no division
by a vanished residual and no strict-PSD assumption. Equivalently,

 |b_cut C_out-F_cut-B_cut-J_forward
                     -mu_query*mu_tail/(2q_cert)|
       <=2 sqrt((I_cut-b_cut F_cut-mu_query^2/(4q_cert))
                 *(1+2s_tail+C_in+J_in-mu_tail^2/(4q_cert))).   (7)

If every edge incident to j vanishes, I_cut=F_cut=B_cut=J_forward=
mu_query=0. Then (5) forces b_cut C_out=0. Since C_out>=2, b_cut=0.
This recovers the qualitative incidence obstruction, now as the zero-cost
case of an exact quantitative condition at every finite certificate level.
If b_cut=0 there is no contradiction; the zero-step case is included.

For an alternative exact q cost define

 Z_cut=[ A_cut K_cut/2 ; K_cut/2 D_cut ],
 m_cut=(mu_query,mu_tail)^T.

Condition (4) is equivalent to Z_cut>=0, m_cut in Range(Z_cut), and

 q_cert >= (1/4) m_cut^T Z_cut^+ m_cut.                         (8)

Here Z_cut^+ inverts its positive eigenvalues and vanishes on its kernel.
For proof, diagonalize the real symmetric Z_cut. A component of m_cut
on a zero eigenvector would give an unbounded linear polynomial in that
free direction. Once these components vanish, complete one square for
each positive eigenvalue. This proves necessity and sufficiency, including
all singular cases. If 4A_cut D_cut-K_cut^2>0, the right side is explicitly

 (D_cut*mu_query^2-K_cut*mu_query*mu_tail+A_cut*mu_tail^2)
                         /(4A_cut D_cut-K_cut^2).             (9)

## 3. Why total cut flow cannot replace its position moment

Here is ONE exact supplied certificate. Take n=2, h=(1,1), q_cert=1/5,
with the following nonzero multipliers and every other multiplier zero:

 lambda_02=1, lambda_10=1, lambda_21=2,
 lambda_(1,star)=1, lambda_(2,star)=1.                          (10)

All coefficients are nonnegative. The finite flows are respectively0,0,2.
With x_1=x_0-g_0 and x_2=x_0-g_0-g_1, direct expansion of (1) gives

 M=(1/5)||x_0-(5/2)(g_1+g_2)||^2
                                      +(3/4)||g_1-g_2||^2.   (11)

For an explicit coefficient check, the sum of the position terms in (10)
is -<x_0,g_1+g_2>-||g_0||^2+<g_0,g_1>+<g_0,g_2>
+3<g_1,g_2>. The norm terms add ||g_0||^2+2||g_1||^2+2||g_2||^2
-<g_0,g_1>-<g_0,g_2>-2<g_1,g_2>. Their sum is exactly the expansion
of (11) after adding q_cert||x_0||^2. This is full PSD with the
original independent g_0 present as a kernel direction.

For j=0, T_cut={1,2}, the exact data are

 b_cut=1, s_tail=1, C_in=1, C_out=3,
 F_cut=B_cut=I_cut=1, J_forward=J_in=1,
 mu_query=0, mu_tail=2,
 A_cut=0, D_cut=5, K_cut=0.

Thus (4) is the singular rank-one square (z-5v)^2/5, and (8) gives
the exact cost1/5. Dropping the target-position moment J_in would assert

 mu_tail^2 <= 4q_cert(1+2s_tail+C_in),                          (12)

which here reads4<=16/5, false. Equivalently, that proposed scalar cost
would falsely require q_cert>=1/4. Dropping J_forward from K_cut likewise
produces K_cut=1 while A_cut=0, falsely rejecting the same full certificate.
Therefore unweighted cross-cut flow cannot be substituted in these exact
capacity tests. The actual remote edge0->2, whose target lies one unit
of step mass into the tail, supplies the missing moment. This refutes
(12), not every conceivable scalar invariant or an existential re-selection.

## 4. A general interval restriction and an explicit two-cut test

Partition {0,...,n} into m nonempty chronological interval blocks
B_0,...,B_(m-1), including n in the final block. Let

 H_r=sum_(i in B_r,i<n)h_i,
 ell_r(i)=sum_(t in B_r,t<i)h_t for i in B_r,
 Lambda_rs=sum_(i in B_r,j in B_s)lambda_ij for r!=s,
 Lambda_(r,star)=sum_(i in B_r)lambda_(i,star)=mu_r,
 Lambda_(star,r)=sum_(i in B_r)lambda_(star,i),
 E_rs=sum_(i in B_r,j in B_s)ell_s(j)lambda_ij for r!=s,
 E_(star,r)=sum_(j in B_r)ell_r(j)lambda_(star,j),
 in_r^cross=sum_(a!=r)Lambda_ar,

where the last sum includes a=star. E is a target-position FIRST MOMENT,
not an arbitrary error term. Only off-diagonal block data are needed.
Temporarily write E_rr for the analogous internal sum during the proof.
Set every g_i=u_r on B_r, while leaving x_0=z independent.

The complete restricted polynomial is

 q_cert||z||^2-sum_r mu_r<z,u_r>
             +sum_r D_r^blk||u_r||^2+sum_(r<s)K_rs^blk<u_r,u_s>,  (13)

where

 D_r^blk=(1+2H_r)*1_(r=m-1)
       +sum_(a!=r)(Lambda_ar+E_ar)-H_r sum_(s>r)Lambda_rs,     (14)

 K_rs^blk=H_r(mu_s+sum_(t<=r)Lambda_st)
       -H_s sum_(t>s)Lambda_rt
       -Lambda_rs-Lambda_sr-E_rs-E_sr       (r<s).             (15)

The sums in (15) have only finite block indices; all appearances of the
optimum in its position term are in mu_s. These identities hold even
for arbitrary flow arrays before imposing PSD, and every original edge
is included through these exact sums.

Proof. Write p_i=sum_(a<r)H_a u_a+ell_r(i)u_r for i in B_r, so
x_i=z-p_i. Position terms equal

 -sum_r mu_r<z,u_r>
 +sum_r sum_(i in B_r)out_i<u_r,p_i>
 -sum_(r,s finite)sum_(i in B_r,j in B_s)lambda_ij<u_r,p_j>.

For ||u_r||^2 the local part of the first sum is
sum_(i in B_r)ell_r(i)out_i. By node flow (2) this equals
sum_a E_ar+2H_r*1_(r=m-1), with a including r and star. The second
sum subtracts E_rr and H_r sum_(s>r)Lambda_rs. The cross norm coefficient
is (out_r^cross+in_r^cross)/2=in_r^cross+1_(r=m-1). Combining proves(14).

For r<s, sources in B_r contribute
-H_s sum_(t>s)Lambda_rt-E_rs to the coefficient of <u_r,u_s>.
Sources in B_s contribute
H_r(out_s-sum_(t>r)Lambda_st)-E_sr
=H_r(mu_s+sum_(t<=r)Lambda_st)-E_sr.
The norm terms contribute -Lambda_rs-Lambda_sr. Their sum is(15).

Let Z_blk be the m by m matrix with diagonal D_r^blk and off-diagonal
K_rs^blk/2, and let mu_blk=(mu_0,...,mu_(m-1))^T. Necessarily

 Z_blk>=0, mu_blk in Range(Z_blk),
 q_cert >= (1/4)mu_blk^T Z_blk^+ mu_blk.                       (16)

This follows from the same elementary square completion proved for(8).
It is EXACT for the chosen restricted subspace, hence only necessary for
the full certificate unless every block is a singleton.

For a concrete TWO-CUT statement take B_0={0,...,k-1},
B_1={k,...,l-1}, B_2={l,...,n}, where1<=k<l<=n. Then

 D_0^blk=sum_(a!=0)(Lambda_a0+E_a0)-H_0(Lambda_01+Lambda_02),
 D_1^blk=sum_(a!=1)(Lambda_a1+E_a1)-H_1 Lambda_12,
 D_2^blk=1+2H_2+sum_(a!=2)(Lambda_a2+E_a2),
 K_01^blk=H_0(mu_1+Lambda_10)-H_1 Lambda_02
                              -Lambda_01-Lambda_10-E_01-E_10,
 K_02^blk=H_0(mu_2+Lambda_20)-Lambda_02-Lambda_20-E_02-E_20,
 K_12^blk=H_1(mu_2+Lambda_20+Lambda_21)
                              -Lambda_12-Lambda_21-E_12-E_21. (17)

The diagonal sums include star. Define
d_r=D_r^blk-mu_r^2/(4q_cert) and
c_rs=K_rs^blk/2-mu_r*mu_s/(4q_cert).
Full PSD implies all d_r>=0, c_rs^2<=d_r*d_s, and the additional
coupled two-cut condition

 d_0 d_1 d_2+2c_01 c_02 c_12
              -d_0 c_12^2-d_1 c_02^2-d_2 c_01^2>=0.          (18)

Conversely these principal-minor conditions make the restricted3 by3
Schur matrix PSD. To justify the singular cases without a theorem black
box: if some d_r=0, its two pair tests force both adjacent c entries0
and the remaining2 by2 criterion suffices. If all d_r>0, eliminate the
first diagonal; the remaining2 by2 matrix has nonnegative diagonal from
the pair tests and determinant det(original)/d_0>=0. Square completion
then proves PSD. Thus(18) retains the simultaneous two-cut correlation,
including degenerate kernels, rather than checking two separate cuts.

As a normalization check, the ONE-BLOCK case of(14) has S=sum h_i,
nu_i=lambda_(star,i), nu=sum_i nu_i, mu=2+nu, and

 D=1+2S+sum_i(1+sum_(t<i)h_t)nu_i
                  <=1+2S+(1+S)nu.

Consequently q_cert>=mu^2/(4D)>=1/(1+2S). The last inequality is exactly

 (1+2S)(2+nu)^2-4(1+2S+(1+S)nu)
                      =(1+2S)nu^2+4S nu>=0.

This recovers the familiar Huber bound without incorrectly suppressing
optimum-source edges at q_cert>Huber. The empty horizon is m=1,H_0=0.

## 5. What does and does not close under another cut

There IS an exact matrix update with explicitly retained boundary data.
Merge neighboring blocks r,s=r+1 into B. For an outside source block a,

 H_B=H_r+H_s, mu_B=mu_r+mu_s,
 Lambda_aB=Lambda_ar+Lambda_as,
 E_aB=E_ar+E_as+H_r Lambda_as,
 Lambda_Ba=Lambda_ra+Lambda_sa,
 E_Ba=E_ra+E_sa.                                              (19)

The formulas for a=star use the same target moment. They follow by
shifting every target in B_s by H_r in its new local elapsed coordinate.
Algebraically merging means setting u_r=u_s in(13), so
D_B^blk=D_r^blk+D_s^blk+K_rs^blk and
K_Ba^blk=K_ra^blk+K_sa^blk, with symmetric interpretation of K.
The matrix is a congruence of the finer matrix. A further split requires
the actual finer Lambda and E data; it cannot be recovered from(19) by
knowing only aggregate totals. Conditions become no weaker under refinement
because the tested formal subspace increases.

One may instead eliminate a block variable by minimizing(13). If D_r^blk>0,
the exact Schur update on the other blocks is

 q_new=q_cert-mu_r^2/(4D_r^blk),
 mu_s,new=mu_s-mu_r K_rs^blk/(2D_r^blk),
 D_s,new=D_s^blk-(K_rs^blk)^2/(4D_r^blk),
 K_st,new=K_st^blk-K_rs^blk K_rt^blk/(2D_r^blk).               (20)

If D_r^blk=0, PSD requires mu_r=0 and every K_rs^blk=0, so this is a
zero row and can be removed without division. These claims follow by
completing D_r^blk||u_r-[mu_r z-sum_(s!=r)K_rs^blk u_s]/(2D_r^blk)||^2.
They preserve PSD as matrix statements. They need not preserve nonnegative
optimum flow or the normalized full-objective certificate class.

Here is an exact supplied FAMILY showing that failure. For any b_long>2,
take n=1,h_0=b_long,

 q_cert=b_long^2-2b_long+2,
 lambda_(0,star)=lambda_(star,0)=2b_long^2,
 lambda_(1,star)=2,

and every other coefficient0. The flows are0,2 and every coefficient is
nonnegative. Its complete full-space identity is

 M=2b_long^2||g_0-x_0/2+g_1/(2b_long)||^2
                              +(1/2)||g_1+(b_long-2)x_0||^2. (21)

Expanding gives q_cert||x_0||^2-2b_long^2<x_0,g_0>-2<x_0,g_1>
+2b_long^2||g_0||^2+2b_long<g_0,g_1>+||g_1||^2, which is exactly(1)
for the stated multipliers. Thus there is no feasibility or duality
assumption hidden in this example. Eliminating g_0 yields the last square
in(21), whose effective optimum coefficient is

 mu_1,new=2-b_long<0.                                         (22)

In a genuine full certificate on the remaining vectors, the coefficient
of -<x_0,g_1> must be the nonnegative actual lambda_(1,star).
Thus this Schur elimination does NOT close in that cone. It also changes
the initial-distance coefficient to (b_long-2)^2/2. A purported exact
constant-preserving step cannot discard these changes. The example refutes
automatic direct Schur heredity, not the possibility of another nonnegative
re-selection at the same original q_cert.

The interval state (H,Lambda,E,mu,Z_blk) therefore closes algebraically
under coarsening or refinement WHEN the actual finer data are retained.
It is not a fixed-dimensional scalar capacity state. At singleton blocks
ell_r=0, the substitution is the identity on all original gradients; then
(16), together with nonnegative multipliers and flow, is exactly the
original full-certificate feasibility problem. Merely refining all the way
to that matrix is a change of coordinates, not a solution of the profile.

## 6. Precise implication for W and the remaining obstacle

For a fixed partition, or a chosen collection of partitions, define L_test(h)
as the infimum of q>0 over all finite nonnegative arrays with the original
flow(2) that pass the corresponding exact restricted matrices. Every true
full certificate is feasible for that relaxation. In particular the attained
certificate at q=W_n(h), supplied by accepted1252b8823bb5a290 including
all zero steps, is feasible. Therefore

                         W_n(h)>=L_test(h).                  (23)

This direction is justified by that explicit accepted attainment theorem,
not by an unspecified conic strong-duality slogan. No attainment of the
relaxed infimum is asserted or needed. A lower bound on q for just one
selected array would not prove(23) with that particular array's value.

To prove the affirmative target using these tests one must still establish,
for every epsilon>0 and all sufficiently large n, uniformly in h>=0,
that their feasible flow/moment states cost at least(1-epsilon)/B_n.
Neither(6), (18), nor(19) proves that estimate. A bounded-state separator
dominated by the exact primitive/dominant F/G recursions has not been found.
Dropping moments is disproved by(10); direct Schur cone closure is disproved
by(21). Retaining the entire singleton state leaves a theorem equivalent to
the original minimax comparison. That closure route is BLOCKED, not routine.

Deliverable of this round: a quantitative single queried-cut necessity,
an explicit simultaneous two-cut determinant with all actual moments,
their exact singular/zero-step rules, and two scoped supplied-certificate
obstructions to unjustified scalar closure. The all-horizon target remains
unresolved; no new accepted fact or actual asymptotic match is claimed.
